Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any block module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 569. The projective resolution has length at most 826 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to block theory, we decompose the problem into 569 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 826. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.

Theorem 212.1001 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The block variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 535 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 335 and intersection form of rank 3.
